## Document 048: 2330台積電 群益

Source PDF: all report pdf/2330台積電 群益.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-14
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

1 群益投顧 
ASML (ASML US) 與台積電 (2330 TT) 共同推動 High NA EUV 將自現行的 6 吋光罩，
轉移至更大的 12 吋 光 罩。 目標於 2031 年之前建立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ， 並 於
2033 年之前實現微影系統全面就緒。台積電並表示自 2030 年 起 ， 將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產
中導入 ASML 的 High NA  EUV 。台積電與 ASML 共同發起 6 吋光罩轉移至 12 吋光
罩 的 規 格 轉 移 ， 其 戰 略 意 義 在 於 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積 電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。 因 此
對 台 積 電 的 投 資 評 等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

ASML 與 台 積 電 共 同 推 動 High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋 光 罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大
的 12 吋 光 罩 ：  
09/07/2026 ASML 與 台 積 電 於 BACUS 國 際 光 學 工 程 學 會(SPIE) 先 進 微 影 技
術 研 討 會 前 宣 布 發 起 產 業 合 作 ， 共 同 推 動High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋光
罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大 的 12 吋 光 罩 ， 預 期 將 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 的 生 產 力 、 降 低 晶
片 製 造 成 本 ， 並 消 除 需 要 2 張 6 吋 光 罩 在 High NA EUV 曝 光 時 進 行 拼 接 的
限 制，使 整 個 半 導 體 產 業 受 惠。此 轉 移 能 讓 先 進 晶 片 製 造 服 務 提 供 者 充 分 利
用 High NA EUV 的 優 勢 ， 更 提 升 未 來 世 代 先 進 晶 片 的 成 本 效 益 。 
目標於 2031 年 之 前 建 立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ，並於 2033 年 之 前 實
現 微 影 系 統 全 面 就 緒 ， 以 支 援 先 進 製 程 量 產 。 台 積 電 並 表 示 自2030 年 起 ，
將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產 中 導 入 ASML 的 High NA 技 術 。  
12 吋光罩 使 High NA 效 率 提 升 ：  
現行 EUV 設備的 NA(Numerical Aperture ， NA) 為 0.33 ， High NA 則 提 高 到
0.55 。 NA 數 值 越 高 ， 解 析 度 也 越 高 、 曝 光 時 能 呈 現 的 細 節 愈 多 。ASML 資
料顯示 High NA 可 將 最 小 解 析 尺 寸 從 現 行 EUV 的約 13 奈 米 進 一 步 縮 小 到
8 奈 米 ， 也 就 是 能 在 晶 圓 上 印 出 更 細 微 的 電 路 圖 形 。 部 分 原 本 需 要2 次 甚 至
更多次曝光才能完成的圖形，也有機會改以單次曝光處理，降低製程複雜
度 、 缺 陷 風 險 與 生 產 周 期 。 
舉 例 來 說 ，0.33 NA EUV 機 台 ， 至 少 需 要 4 張 光 罩 、 分 多 次 曝 光 才 能 完 成
A14 、 A10 金 屬 層 圖 形 ；High NA EUV ， 只 要 1 張 光 罩 就 能 完 成 相 同 圖 形 。
但 解 析 度 提 高 ，High NA 的 光 學 設 計 讓 單 次 可 曝 光 範 圍 縮 小 到 原 本EUV 的
50% ， 製 作 大 顆 晶 片 時 可 能 必 須 分 成2 次 曝 光 ， 再 把 圖 形 拼 接 起 來 。 這 也 是
台積電與 ASML 此 次 推 動 12 吋 光 罩 的 原 因 ， 相 較 現 行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 放 大 光 罩
尺 寸 預 期 能 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 生 產 力、降 低 晶 片 製 造 成 本，並 消 除 High NA
曝 光 時 的 拼 接 限 制 。 
台 積 電 預 計 2030 年 開 始 使 用 High NA  EUV ， 推估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或
A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採 用：  
台積電預計 2030 年 開 始 導入 High NA  EUV ，比 12 吋 光 罩 微 影 系 統 預 計 全
面就緒的 2033 年 更 早 。 這 代 表 台 積 電 初 期 導 入High NA 時 ， 仍 會 先 使 用 現
行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 之 後 再 隨 12 吋 光 罩 與 相 關 系 統 成 熟 逐 步 轉 換 。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

2 群益投顧 
根 據 台 積 電 目 前 公 開 的 製 程 藍 圖 ，A13 與 A12 製 程 都 預 計 2029 年 量 產 。 其
中 A13 是 A14 平 台 的 延 伸 ，A12 則 將 超 級 電 軌(Super Power Rail) 背面供電
技術導入 A14 平 台 。 因 此 N2 、 A16 、 A14 、 A13 與 A12 都 早 於 台 積 電 預 計
2030 年 導 入 High NA ， 推 估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或 A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採
用。  
Intel (INTC US) 和 Samsung (005930 KS) 領 先 台 積 電 採 用 High NA EUV ，但
也 同 時 參 與 12 吋 光 罩 開 發 ：  
Intel 在 2024 年 宣 布 完 成 全 球 首 套 商 用 High NA EUV 設 備 的 組 裝 ；2026 年
High NA 已 經 正 式 進 入 量 產 ， 並 用 於 部 分 18A 製 程 層 。 是 目 前 High NA 導
入 進 度 最 快 的 晶 片 製 造 商 。Samsung 則預計 2028 年導入 DRAM 量 產 ； 台 積
電選擇在 2030 年 導 入 。Intel 和 Samsung 也 同 時 表 示 與 ASML 及 供 應 鏈 合
作 ， 推 動 未 來 轉 向 大 尺 寸 光 罩 。 
台 積 電 與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，將 領 導 業
界 規 格 ：  
6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋光罩 的 這 項 升 級 非 單 純 的 尺 寸 放 大 ， 而 是 面 臨 生 態 系
與 物 理 極 限 的 多 重 挑 戰 。 整 體 供 應 鏈 重 構 ， 包 括 基 板 與 材 料 以 及 設 備 等 等 。
12 吋 光 罩 石 英 基 板 的 平 坦 度 、 缺 陷 密 度 與 熱 膨 脹 係 數 控 制 難 度 倍 增 。 製 程
設 備 如 薄 膜 沉 積、蝕 刻、清 洗 與 自 動 化 搬 運 設 備 皆 需 重 新 開 發。後 續 檢 測 與
量測極限檢測難度也增加，更大面積的圖形缺陷檢測（ Inspection ）與修補
技 術 需 要 突 破 。 在 12 吋 面 積 上 維 持 奈 米 級 的 對 位 精 度 與 線 寬 均 勻 性 極 為 困
難   
電 子 設 計 自 動 化 (EDA) 工 具 必 須 重 新 適 應 大 尺 寸 曝 光 視 場 與 資 料 處 理 。 12 
吋 光 罩 的 圖 形 檔 案 與 運 算 複 雜 度 將 大 幅 上 升 。 
台積電與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，其 戰略意
義在於 ，台 積 電 為 全 球 最 大 晶 圓 代 工 廠，為 先 進 製 程 最 大 的 供 應 商，可 以 領
導 光 罩 基 底 供 應 商 、 光 罩 檢 測 設 備 商， 甚 至 EDA 廠 等 整 個 生 態 系 ，除 共同
分攤 ， 跨 越 升 級 12 吋 光 罩 的 龐 大 研 發 成 本外 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積
電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。因 此 對 台 積 電 的投 資 評 等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

### Page 3

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

## Document 055: 2548華固 第一金

楊凱云 
(886-2)2563-1666 
pineapple.yang@firstsec.com.tw

華固(2548) 
買進, TP 111 (潛在報酬 18.59%)  
買進 
Price(09/17):93.6 
Target Price：111

展望 2026年，完工案量與前一年相當，憑藉優異銷售策略與品牌
聲量，住宅案已全數完銷，商辦則持續與潛在買方洽談中，若期限
內未能找到整棟購置客戶，將改採分層銷售模式，預計年底前去化
約 50%，後續若順利完成整棟出售，則不排除上修獲利預估。展
望後市，公司持續獵地推案，目前建案能見度至 2034年，銷售中
總銷逾千億元，待推出總銷逾 900 億元，顯示公司中長期業績成
長無虞，惟短期受房市景氣及銷售速度放緩影響，業績表現仍存在
不確定性。股利方面，公司過往現金股利最低配發5元，且現金股
利配發率長期維持 70~80%，推估 2027 年現金股利有望達 6~7
元。綜合以上，研究部預估 2026、2027年 EPS 7.28、9.78元，
以 1.4*PBR評價，給予目標價 111元，投資建議為買進。 
 近期投資評等  營運概況 
1H26 營收 57.07億元，YoY+181.4%，稅後損益 9.43 億元，
YoY+307%，EPS 2.92 元，略低於研究部預期。上半年營收
主要來自新案「華固上文林」持續交屋挹注，全案總銷 61 億
元，已順利於8M26 完成過戶。獲利方面，1H26 毛利率 27%，
YoY-5.8ppt，由於新推案土地成本較高，加上興建期間物料及
工資上漲，導致利潤率略低於預期。

 公司展望 
展望 2026 年 ， 全年將迎來三大建案完工交屋 ， 合計總銷為179
億元，YoY-2.8%，分別為「華固一莊」(總銷 41 億元，完銷，
8M26 交屋)、「華固上文林」(總銷 61 億元，完銷，1H26交
屋)、「華固時代置地」(總銷 77 億元，持續與潛在客戶洽談
整棟出售，若期限內未能找到整棟購置客戶，公司將改採分層
銷售模式，預計年底前去化約 50%)。獲利方面，住宅案因土
地成本墊高，個案毛利率應介於25~26%之間；商辦產品則因
市場降溫，成交價格與利潤率回歸合理水準，毛利率預估約
28~30%，使全年合併毛利率將較 2025 年下滑，預估為
26.8%，YoY-1.43ppt。綜合以上，研究部預估 2026 年營收
140.85 億元， YoY-22.77%，稅後損益 23.27 億元，
Yo-28.21%，EPS 7.28元。

展望 2027 年，預計有三大建案完工交屋，合計總銷為205 億

預計 3Q27 完工交屋)、「華固豐匯」(總銷 47 元，銷售率
35~40%，4Q27 完工交屋)、「華固慕川」(總銷 50 億元，銷
售率 95~100%，4Q27 完工交屋)。由於台中地區銷售動能相
對疲弱，研究部認為「華固頂匯」及「華固豐匯」於完工前達
成完銷仍有一定難度，加上兩案完工時程接近年底，實際入帳
時程仍具不確定性，故預估整體入帳率約 60%，若加計「華
固時代置地」順利完銷，全年營收有望挑戰 160 億元大關。
綜合以上，研究部預估 2027 年營收 166.3 億元，
YoY+18.06%，稅後損益31.28 億元，YoY+34.43%，EPS 9.78
元。

 推案計畫及銷售中個案概況 
銷售中個案： 「華固頂匯」總銷108 億元，銷售率70~80%(僅
開放 200 戶銷售，剩餘 60 戶預計採成屋銷售)；「華固豐匯」
總銷 47 億元，銷售率 35~40%(出現少量退戶)；「華固慕川」
總銷 50 億元，銷售率95~100%； 「華固織幸」總銷33 億元，
銷售率 95~100%； 「吾雙」總銷67 億元，銷售率95~100%、
「華固芙心」 總銷53 億元，銷售率95~100%、 「華固四季匯」
總銷 54 億元，銷售率35~40%、「華固譽誠」總銷54 億元，
銷售率 85~90%、「華固創匯園區」總銷 325 億元，銷售率
25~30%。

2027年公司將再推出 「央北二案」(總銷64億元 ， 單價105~110
萬/坪，總價 4~5 千萬)、「松勇路案」(總銷 70億元，250 萬
/坪，2~3 億/戶，合計總銷為 134 億元，預計於 2030~2031
年完工交屋。

市況方面，2H24 起國內房市受限貸令及信用管制措施影響，
台中地區產品銷售速度趨緩，雙北地區則相對穩健，因此公司
未來獵地方向將回歸雙北，並以 60%住宅+40%廠辦為目標。
隨房市移轉量及房價已明顯降溫，因此央行近期放寬第二戶貸
款成數至 7 成，有助於提振換屋需求及購屋信心，最壞情況已
過，整體銷售動能有望回溫。

土地庫存方面，尚有逾 900 億元待推案將陸續於 2027~2030
年推出，並於 2034 年前完工挹注，顯示公司中長期業績成長

 結論與建議
展望 2026 年，完工案量與前一年相當，憑藉優異銷售策略與
品牌聲量，住宅案已全數完銷，商辦則持續與潛在買方洽談
中，若期限內未能找到整棟購置客戶，將改採分層銷售模式，
預計年底前去化約 50%，後續若順利完成整棟出售，則不排
除上修獲利預估。展望後市，公司持續獵地推案，目前建案能
見度至 2034 年，銷售中總銷逾千億元，待推出總銷逾900 億
元，顯示公司中長期業績成長無虞，惟短期受房市景氣及銷售
速度放緩影響，業績表現仍存在不確定性。股利方面，公司過
往現金股利最低配發 5 元，且現金股利配發率長期維持
70~80%，推估 2027年現金股利有望達 6~7 元。綜合以上，
研究部預估 2026、2027年 EPS 7.28、9.78元，以 1.4*PBR
評價，給予目標價 111 元，投資建議為買進。

## Document 061: 260914_gs_repon

Source PDF: all report pdf/260914_gs_repon.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-14
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

We hosted Repon’s management at our Asia Leaders Conference 2026 (Aug 31 - Sep 
2) in Hong Kong. Management remains positive on their servers rail kits business, 
driven by both general servers and AI servers, riding on the generative AI trend. The 
company’s positive tone echoes our positive view on AI servers supply chain, on 
which we raised our AI servers implied AI chips forecast by 14% / 22% / 14% in 
2026E-28E in June (report link) to +75% / 42% / 20% YoY in 2026E-28E, or 19m / 
27m / 32m in 2026E-28E. Capex estimates on leading US CSPs was also raised by 8% 
/ 27% / 25% in 2026E-28E to +76% / +35% / +8% YoY in 2026E-28E, or up to 
US$1.1tn by 2028E (report link). As we highlighted in our FII Chairman and Innolight 
Chairman visits takeaways, supply chain remains positive on rising AI investments in 
coming years, with spending across CSP , NeoCloud, Government, and Enterprise, and 
we are at an early stage of infrastructure spending and application development. 
End-customers are involved more in supply chain product development and capacity 
investment, also better reflecting real demand.  
Company profile: Repon (6584.TWO, not covered) is a leading rail kit supplier for AI 
servers, general servers, and home appliances. The company provides 2U rail kits for 
server clients, and has been gradually expanding its product line-up to 4U / 7U rail 
kits. As the early entrant of server rail kits, Repon has built deep partnerships with 
US CSP clients by offering customized services. 
Key Takeaways

1. General servers rail kits outlook: Management stated servers revenues 
contribution came down in 2Q26 vs. 1Q26 due to raw material shortage affecting 
customers’ procurement on rail kits for general servers; however, management sees 
their major CSP customer’s procurement recovering in 3Q26, and the next largest 
customer (also a leading US CSP) procurement is also recovering in September. The 
rail kits for next generation of general servers (powered by Intel’s Birch Stream 
platform) will also start shipment in 3Q26, supporting the revenues growth. 
2. AI servers rail kits outlook: Management remains positive and stated their rail

### Page 2

kits for the next generation AI server racks powered by NVIDIA is entering shipment in
late 3Q26, so is the rail kits for the next generation ASIC AI servers (air-cooling version)
for their major customer, and the rail kits for the liquid-cooling version should also start
shipment in 4Q26 with a majority ramp up in 1Q27. The rail kits for storage servers could
also show contribution in 1Q27, followed by rail kits for more ASIC AI servers, supporting

the growth ahead.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
We keep our 3Q26 iPhone build estimate unchanged at 54M units (+4% q/q, -2%
y/y). The slower y/y run rate reflects new model SKUs shifting to the premium
segment (18 Pro/Pro Max and iPhone Fold), while the iPhone 18 model is scheduled

for introduction in 1H27. We introduce our 4Q26 iPhone build estimate at 73M
units (+35% q/q, -4% y/y). Total new iPhone 18 model family units reached 71M
units (-21% y/y) in 2H26, with one less SKU launched compared to last year. We
expect 7M units of iPhone Duo builds in 2H26. A starting price of US$1,999 (relative
to expectations of US$2,300 or higher) appears favorable to builds, but there are
likely still some production issues that the supply chain is working on. This is why

the iPhone Duo is available to  the market only one month later, and we will
continue to monitor ramp-up progress for any upside or downside to builds. Our full
year iPhone build estimate is 235M units (+3% y/y).
Our 3Q26 iPad build forecast is unchanged at 12M units (-8% q/q, -14% y/y). We
attribute the 3Q sequential decline to some pull-forward demand in 1H and the
impact of rising prices. We introduce our 4Q iPad build forecast at 11M units (-8%

q/q, -21% y/y). Our full year iPad build estimate is 46.1M units (-15% y/y).

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
Monthly Databook: Aug-26
Shipments -19% vs. MSe;
Lowering 3Qe by 7%,
Introducing 4Qe Builds
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Howard Kao
Equity Analyst
Howard.Kao@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2989
Irene Yen
Research Associate
Irene.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2869
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+

Andy Meng, CFA
Equity Analyst
Andy.Meng@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7689
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Overall August ODM notebook shipments came in 19% below our estimate: Pull-
forward demand appears to have come to an end, channel partners and distributors
have stocked up on inventory, and OEMs are slowing their sales into the channel.
We believe this is the main reason why August NB builds came in slower than our

expectation. However, we continue to expect product mix to continue to shift
toward higher-end, more-profitable models. Several ODMs such as Quanta and
Compal have even lowered 3Q NB build forecasts compared to their earlier guidance
on 2Q earnings calls three weeks ago. Thus we lower our 3Q NB build estimate by
7% to 25.4M units (-16% q/q, -25% y/y). This sequential decline is sub-seasonal vs.
the 15-year average seasonality of +5% q/q in 3Q, and implies September NB builds

at 9.9M units (+35% m/m, -18% y/y).
We introduce our 4Q notebook build estimate at 23.9M units (-6% q/q, -26% y/
y): This sequential decline is sub-seasonal (despite an already lowered base in 3Q)
vs. the 15-year average seasonality of flat q/q in 4Q. None of the ODMs have
provided official guidance for 4Q notebook builds, but our preliminary checks
suggests that they will continue to fall q/q. On our estimate, this implies that the
top 5 Taiwan NB ODMs' builds for CY26 will come in at ~108.8M units (-16% y/

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Top five ODMs' August 2026 NB shipments were 7.3M units (-11% m/m, -35% y/y),
19% below our estimate.
We see September 2026 NB builds at 9.9M units (+35% m/m, -18% y/y).
We lower our 3Q26 NB build estimate by 7% to 25.4M units (-16% q/q, -25% y/y).
We introduce our 4Q26 NB build estimate at 23.9M units (-6% q/q, -26% y/y).

Our top five ODMs' CY26 NB build estimate is ~108.8M units (-16% y/y).
September 14, 2026 12:00 AM GMT

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

ab 14 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
APAC Tech
TW Summit Day 1 on Hardware/Components
Chassis/rail kit suppliers have started to ramp for new server platform
One chassis supplier has noted that, post solid inventory built in Q2, ODMs have slowed
down pull-in momentum for one of the major US CSP ASIC suppliers (likely AWS, in our
view) in Q3 due to supply bottleneck for memory and cooling components. Inventories
are anticipated to be digested by September and anticipate ODM pull-in momentum to

resume in September/October. Another supplier noted AWS general server volumes
were also down in Q2 on component mismatch (citing memory, CPU, and MLCC
shortages), but seeing shipment coming back from Q3. Meanwhile, one supplier noted
it has begun to ramp chassis shipments for NVDA Vera Rubin platform with enterprise
started pull-in earlier in Q226, followed by major CSPs' orders in Q326; and another
supplier noted it anticipates to start shipping VR rail kits in volumes  in Q326. Both

suppliers noted they have witnessed stronger than expected strength for NVDA GB as
well as HGX platform in H126; and picking up strong momentum from enterprise
customers as well. The supply chain continues to point to robust AI server growth and
healthy general server demand into H226; consistent with UBS expectations for King
Slide and Chenbro AI server volumes to take off strongly into Sep/Q426, backed by their
leading positions in key GPU and ASIC platforms, with favourable content uplifts.

Other highlights from the day one hardware meetings
1) ZDT anticipates optical module and server business will strengthen from Q226 and
more than doubled in 2026 (optical module/server/IC substrate sales mix  up from 12%
in 2025 to 20%+/45-50% in 2026/2030F). It is building out its high-end PCB capacity,
investing NT$80bn in 2026 with more strategic investments in 2027/2028 to focus on
iHDI/mSAP, HLC, IC substrates, and advanced FPCs, with production to come online in

phases through 2030. 2) Nan Juen noted Eagle Stream remain the mainstream this year
but Birch Stream platform has started to take off in Q2/Q3. Small volumes of Vera Rubin
rail kits started in Q3  with potential ASIC project to kick off in Q4. 3) Chenbro reiterated
+40% YoY 2026 sales target and remained optimistic on 2027 outlook  supported by
strong chassis momentum from both CSPs/enterprises and rack business expansion
(guiding rack sales contribution from 10-15% in 2026 to 15%+ in 2027F). 4)  Lenovo

targets to outgrow its view for 10%-20% YoY industry PC unit decline while data center
business is seeing strong momentum from both CSP and ESMBs with strong AI order
backlog and ISG restructuring move demonstrating strong margin expansion. 5)
Compal sees stable general server demand and AI server picking up streams from
neocloud and enterprise customers while seeing some progress with hyperscalers. It
expects 2026 PC shipments to decline mid teens YoY with consumer models under

pressure into H226 and seeing de-spec on SSD/DRAM for price-sensitive products. 6)
Inventec saw strong growth across AI servers (B300 and higher rack business) and
general servers including two top ASIC CPU projects; and expects NB sales to grow
double digits YoY in 2026F as it volumes outgrow industry (down HSD YoY vs. market
units down teens) on higher commercial exposure (60-70% mix) and higher ASPs.
Stock implications
We stay positive on hardware/component suppliers that commands leading position

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Investing in the technology sector involves a high degree of risk. Rapid technological changes,
increasing competition and exposure to macroeconomic cycles are among the many risks
faced by investors in technology stocks. Moreover, it is extremely difficult to project the
financial results of tech companies since their operating models are highly volatile and
unpredictable. Finally, valuing technology stocks can prove challenging, as neither traditional

nor non-traditional valuation measures have provided much insight into how these stocks
trade. Valuation methodologies in the sector include target price-book-multiples based on
the long term average ROE and the cost of equity.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.
EXCEPTIONS AND SPECIAL CASES:UK and European Investment Fund ratings and definitions are: Buy:  Positive on factors
such as structure, management, performance record, discount; Neutral: Neutral on factors such as structure, management,
performance record, discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record, discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

ab 14 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
WIN Semiconductors
2026 Taiwan Summit: Optical and Infrastructure
to drive mid-to-long-term growth
Q326 revenue tracking in line with guidance
WIN Semi attended our 2026 Taiwan Summit on Sep 14th. August revenue of NT
$1,937mn (+2% QoQ and +31%YoY) is tracking at 65% of both UBSe/Street at +12%
QoQ. WIN Semi maintained its Q326F revenue guidance of low-teens QoQ  growth,
mainly driven by optical and infrastructure segments. It noted revenue outlook from

WiFi has improved from a previously flattish QoQ  to sequentially QoQ growth as a result
of stronger router demand, and cellular remains flattish. Management did not provide
Q426 guidance, noting that demand will partly depend on smartphone seasonality,
although it remains more constructive on the growth outlook for aerospace and non-
smartphone optical markets. In terms of  capex, WIN Semi budgeted NT$2-3bn of capex
spending in 2026, primarily for GaN and InP expansion.

Laser diode remains in qualification but photodiode ramp accelerates
Management noted that its CW lasers and EML remains under  qualification with more
meaningful revenue contribution from 2027 and beyond. While WIN Semi is engaging
with a broader customer base for laser diode than for photodiode, management noted
its near-term optical growth should be driven primarily by the ongoing photodiode
ramp, with volumes expected to increase sequentially both MoM and QoQ through

H226F and continue strengthening into 2027F. Management also noted that its 50G
and 100G VCSEL solutions are already in production, providing additional support for
non-smartphone optical growth. As a result, smartphone-related optical is expected to
decline from 60-70% of optical revenue historically to around 50% in Q326F as non-
smartphone optical applications expand. Management maintained its target for AI/
datacom revenue (driver, PIC, laser diode, photodiode, and etc) exposure to reach high-

single digits by end-2026, up from a low-single-digit contribution in 2025.
Rising aerospace content strengthens long-term growth outlook
Management remains constructive on the long-term outlook and its margin profile as
the business mix shifts toward higher-margin optical and aerospace applications.
Aerospace revenue has increased to over 30% of infrastructure revenue (Infrastructure
was 30-35% of total sales in Q226F), driven by expanding LEO deployments, direct-to-

cell connectivity, and rising RF content as additional frequency bands and advanced
phased-array antennas are adopted, as well as migration from GaAs to GaN platform.
Management views aerospace and optical datacom as key medium- to long-term
growth drivers, supporting a more diversified revenue mix and improved margin profile.
Valuation: Maintain Buy rating with a PE-based price target of NT$500
We rate WIN Semi with  BUY and  PT of NT$500, based on 40x 2027-28E average PE. We

believe WIN Semi is well positioned to capture rising infrastructure and optical growth.

Company Description
Established in 1999, WIN Semiconductors is a leading foundry for compound semiconductor
wafers globally, with 43k 6-inch wafer capacity per month as of end-2022. While it offers
different types of compound semiconductors (GaAs, GaN, InP, etc.), the GaAs compound
contributes the majority of its revenue. Its products are used to make signal power amplifiers

that customers use in networking equipment and communication devices, including base
stations and smartphones.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value WIN Semi using PE relative to its historical trading range and peer average.
Investing in WIN Semi involves risks that include, but are not limited to, rapid technological
changes, increasing competition, exposure to macroeconomic cycles and uncertain
enddemand. Slower wireless technology migration and poor performances by customers

could also significantly affect WIN Semi’s fundamental performance. WIN Semi’s LEO satellite
customers may face execution challenges in launching satellites, which will impact WIN
Semi's infrastructure revenue growth outlook. On the other hand, its progress on developing
and qualifying the optical communication laser diodes will have significant impact to its
earnings outlook and market sentiment. Moreover, it is extremely difficult to project the

financial results of tech companies, since their operating models are highly volatile and
unpredictable.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

### Page 3

Taiwan Electronic Components & Equipment
17 September 2026 Citi Research
3
Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
Buy | TP NT$4,350.00 from NT$3,800.00 | Price NT$3,185.00 (17 Sep 26 13:30)
Sequential growth intact, ASIC carrying the upside — Our checks and mgmt.
comments indicate Vera Rubin platform has begun contributing in small volumes
from Sep, with a further ramp expected in 4Q26E. The ongoing ASIC project is now
shifting progressively from air cooling to liquid cooling, while another ASIC project

enters mass production in 4Q26E, together the main growth drivers as new
capacity comes online through the quarter. We now forecast 3Q26E/4Q26E
revenue growth at 18%/12% QoQ. GPM should also improve on a sequential basis,
supported by yield improvement and a rising contribution from server and liquid
cooling products.
ASIC contribution could exceed 50% of liquid cooling revenue in 2027 — With
cold plate demand continuing to run ahead of supply, we expect AVC to undertake

another round of capacity expansion in 2027 to meet rising ASIC demand,
consistent with mgmt. ’s view that ASIC contribution catches up with GPU in 2027.
We also note ongoing discussion around double-sided cold plate adoption, with
several platforms at the R&D stage, which we see as a source of upside
momentum. Rubin Ultra spec remains under development, and we do not expect
the potential TDP adjustments toward a lower level to affect content value, as cold

plate content is driven more by design than by chip TDP itself. On a higher liquid
cooling contribution, we forecast 2027E revenue to grow another 50% YoY, with
GPM to further increase to 34.9% (+2.2ppts YoY).
Implications — We raise our 2026E/27E/28E earnings forecasts by 3%/13%/16% to
reflect the updated outlook, driven mainly by higher cold plate capacity and
shipment assumptions alongside a more constructive view on ASIC liquid cooling
demand. Our revised target price of NT$4,350 (from NT$3,800) remains based on

25x 2027E EPS. Given AVC’s position in the thermal sector and its valuation
following the recent share price pullback, we see a favorable risk-reward profile and
reiterate our Buy rating.

5
Auras Technology (3324.TWO)
Buy | TP NT$1,800.00 from NT$1,550.00 | Price NT$1,385.00 (17 Sep 26 15:00)
Liquid cooling ramp drives sequential acceleration into 4Q26 — Following Aug-
26 revenue, which pulled back from a high base in Jul-26, we believe Auras remains
on track to meet mgmt. ’s earlier guidance of at least 15% QoQ growth in 3Q26E.
Into 4Q26, shipments of Vera Rubin products, including inner/rack manifolds,

together with the start of mass production for liquid cooling version of ASIC
project, should allow Auras to deliver another solid sequential growth. We now
forecast 3Q26E/4Q26E revenue growth of 17%/16% QoQ, with GPM also on an
upward trend, supported by a higher liquid cooling revenue mix.
GPU & ASIC projects broaden the 2027 growth base — Our checks and mgmt.
comments  indicate Auras is still working through cold plate qualification for the
Vera Rubin platform and is now participating in next generation product

development. Given its operating scale, we believe even a modest share gain could
contribute meaningfully to its top line, and we expect the earliest potential
contribution from 1Q27E. Auras should also benefit from rising ASIC liquid cooling
demand, with existing ASIC project shipments to expand above 4Q26E levels and
several further projects now under discussion. We forecast 2027E revenue growth
of 35% YoY, with liquid cooling mix rising from 61% in 2026E to 70% in 2027E and

GPM reaching 31.3% (+1.2ppts YoY).
Implications — We raise our 2026E/27E/28E earnings forecasts by 2%/14%/17% to
reflect the updated outlook, driven mainly by higher cold plate shipment
assumptions alongside a more constructive view on ASIC liquid cooling demand.
Our revised target price of NT$1,800 (from NT$1,550) remains based on 20x 2027E
EPS. Given Auras’ structural growth in the server business, widening liquid cooling
content across both GPU and ASIC platforms, and an optimizing global

manufacturing footprint in Thailand, we view the current valuation as attractive
and reiterate our Buy rating.

Asia Vital Components
Company description
Asia Vital Components (AVC), founded in 1991, is a global leader in thermal
solutions, originally focusing on PCs but now specializing in servers and
networking. With Furukawa Electric (Japan) as its largest shareholder, AVC
provides a comprehensive range of cooling components, including vapor
chambers (VCs), fans, cold plates, CDUs, and manifolds, as well as chassis

and assembly services for PCs and servers, enabling holistic thermal solution
designs and one-stop shop services. AVC's diverse product portfolio supports
various sectors, including consumer IT, cloud infrastructure, transportation,
energy and networking.
Investment strategy
We rate AVC a Buy. AVC is well-positioned for the liquid-cooling transition,
with leadership in cold plates and broad participation across Nvidia and CSP
projects. Its high-volume machining capability, manufacturing uniformity,

and Vietnam-based capacity provide it with a defensible position as
Blackwell and future deployments expand. The company captures a wider
share of the thermal BOM, across coldplates, manifolds, QDs, and CDU
opportunities, providing a multi-layered earnings engine.
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum
and the expansion of its addressable market. Our TP of NT$4,350 is derived
from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified

given the company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment,
rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing value of thermal
content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural
drivers still in place, we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its
historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported by other qualitative factors such as cold-

plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not
been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price
include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2) heightened
price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling
components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.
Auras Technology
Company description
Auras Technology was founded in 1998 and focused on the design and

manufacturing of thermal modules for NB/PCs. It quickly became one of
Taiwan’s top two NB cooling component suppliers. Early products included
heatsinks, fins, heat pipes, and fans for NBs, PCs, and smartphones. As
device form factor evolved, the company invested in new technologies such
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 12

Taiwan Electronic Components & Equipment
17 September 2026 Citi Research
12
as ultra-thin vapor chamber in the 2000s to address the thermal challenges
of increasingly compact consumer electronics. During the 2010s, Auras
expanded into graphic card and server cooling and was an early adopter of
liquid cooling technology. By 2012, the company had begun developing liquid
cooling component and was among the first Taiwanese firms to venture into
AI server liquid cooling.

Investment strategy
We rate Auras a Buy. Auras is emerging as a key liquid-cooling supplier as
hyperscalers expand deployment. The company has secured qualifications for
cold plates and manifolds, opening access to a wider slate of Nvidia and CSP
projects. Thailand-based production offers non-China capacity at a scale
aligned with hyperscaler sourcing plans, allowing Auras to compete more
effectively for next-generation products.
Valuation
We value Auras using a PE-based approach, reflecting its accelerating

earnings trajectory and rising share in liquid cooling market of AI servers. Our
TP of NT$1,800 is based on 20x 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified
given the company’s expanding server revenue base, deeper engagement
with CSP customers, and broader product portfolio across cold plates, CDMs,
and CDUs.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model, but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported in our view by other qualitative factors such

as rapid RVL progress and Thailand expansion, and so a High Risk rating has
not been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target
price include: 1) slower adoption of liquid cooling; 2) heightened price
competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling components;
and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 084: 260917_ms_CCL

Source PDF: all report pdf/260917_ms_CCL.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-17
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
What’s new? EMC (2383.TW) shares fell 7.7% and TUC (6274.TWO) shares declined
6.7% on Sep 17, vs TAIEX +0.8%. Following the sell-off, investors have questioned
whether CPO could disrupt demand for high-end CCL. The concern is that
integrating optical engines alongside the ASIC on the package substrate shortens
high-speed electrical signal paths that would otherwise traverse the printed circuit

board (PCB), potentially reducing the need for ultra-low-Dk/Df, high-speed CCL.
Our thoughts:  This is not a new debate. We previously discussed the potential
implications of CPO for high-speed CCL demand here and here.  We believe this is
particularly relevant for scale-up architectures, where CPO adoption still faces
meaningful challenges related to cost, manufacturing yield, reliability, serviceability,
and thermal management. As such, we believe the risk of a broad-based reduction in

CCL requirements across AI systems before 2028 remains limited.  We would view
CPO-driven pullbacks in CCL stocks as potential opportunities to accumulate.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Co-packaged optics (CPO) is a long-term risk, not an imminent demand
disruption.
While we agree that broader CPO adoption represents a credible longer-term risk
to premium copper-clad laminate (CCL) content, we see limited risk to the
demand outlook over 2026–28.
We see limited near-term impact on CCL demand or our earnings estimates for

EMC and TUC, leaving our premium-CCL supply-tightness thesis and constructive
fundamental view intact.
September 17, 2026 06:58 AM GMT

## Document 090: 260918_ubs_wiwynn

### Page 1

ab 18 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
Wiwynn
Reaffirm the strong   growth through 2027
New platforms support AI strength into 2027 while general server slower
before next ramp in 2027
We maintain our Buy on Wiwynn with a price target of $ 2,470  and also in the
UBS regional most preferred tech list. Amid the market noise, we stay positive on the
company's growth, with catalysts from liquid cooled ASIC servers in initial production
and ramping up further through 2027 and Helios racks on track as a new project

commencing from late 2026E through 2027. We do see general server cooling into Sep
and Q426 to offset the ASIC server ramp before picking up from Q127 as customers
transition to the next-generation Clearwater Forest/Venice platforms.
August sales ahead supported by general server growth
Wiwynn August sales of NT$144.3bn, +22.6% MoM, 50.4% YoY tracked ahead at
74%/73% UBSe/Street of +27%/+29% QoQ estimate in Q3. We believe the growth is
driven mainly driven by pre-build ahead of further price hikes. The company is seeing

tight supply across major component includes memory, CPU and PCB, but the impact on
deliveries has been limited through August. Since Q226, the company has booked
general server memory on its balance sheet rather than through sales and COGs, which
lifted gross margin in Q2. We factor in lower NRE and mix shifting from general server
now the highest margin with the consigned memory toward ASIC to lower GM/OpM off
the higher Q2 base of 9.3/7.3% to 8.8/6.8% in Q3 and 8.7/6.7% in Q4.

Multiple drivers support another strong year in 2027
We maintain 2027 revenue growth +89% YoY to NT$2,491bn, reflecting strength
across ASICs further ramping and rising liquid penetration toward 30% this generation,
GPUs with Helios racks ramping to 6-7k and general server continuing double digit
volume growth. We keep more conservative on Vera Rubin following limited shipments
on GB300 although should begin shipping in small volumes in Q426 and contributing

~2% of sales in 2027.
Reiterate our BUY with  price target of NT$2,470 (7,350 pre-split)
Following the recent 3-1 stock split, all per-share data below are on a post-split basis. We
reiterate our Buy rating and keep our basic EPS of NT$226.4/NT$174.1 reflecting better
margins, intact ASIC and general server ramps and customer AMD project from Q4 into
2027. Our PT of NT$2,470 is based on same 14x P/E on 2027E EPS also with 3% cash
yield (NT$74 cash dividend). We maintain a positive view reflecting Wiwynn's leverage

to ASIC servers, AMD racks, alongside healthy general server demand.

Company Description
Spun off from Wistron in March 2012, Wiwynn designs and manufactures cloud
infrastructure equipment for hyperscaler data centres. Prior to 2015, Wiwynn provided
storage servers and traditional servers. In 2015, Wiwynn began to shift its focus to

manufacturing full rack server for hyperscale data centre clients. Its key clients include US
Facebook (50-60% of total revenue), Microsoft (40-50%) and Amazon. Wiwynn is the
second-largest white-box server manufacturer in terms of shipments. It has production sites
in Mexico, the Czech Republic and China.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's
interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of

shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on PE multiple methodology.
Key downside risks for Wiwynn include: 1) a slowdown in the US CSP capex cycle; 2) margin
compression due to intense competition; and 3) unfavourable FX movement.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

such as structure, management, performance record, discount; Neutral: Neutral on factors such as structure, management,
performance record, discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record, discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Hon Precision (7769.TW)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
2
Hon Precision
Valuation
Our target price of NT$11,000 is based on a target PER multiple of 36x applied to the average of our 2027E/28E EPS estimates.
Our PER target of 35x is set at 1-std above its 1-year PER average, justified by its high share of the AI/HPC testing handler
market, which we estimate could support an earnings CAGR of 77% in 2025-28, higher than its 3-year CAGR at c40% in 2022-

25. Our estimated 77% earning CAGR is also higher than the +40% average (Bloomberg consensus) of its global testing
equipment peers. As its peers have been trading at  average  36x PER on 2027/28 consensus EPS in the past 1 year, we believe
Hon Precision deserves a further re-rating to our target PER of 36x.
Risks
Our quantitative risk rating system, which looks at historical share price volatility, rates Hon Precision High Risk. However, we
are not assigning a High Risk rating as we forecast an earnings CAGR of >75%, significantly stronger than the growth outlook

for its global peers. Also, we note its dominant position in AI/HPC handler market and expanding market TAM opportunity,
which support our constructive view over its growth outlook.
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target price: 1) Global AI demand slowdown  driving weaker
capacity build for semiconductor equipment; 2) unexpected share losses at its major AI projects; 3) slower-than-expected
capacity ramp at its new facilities, or the company taking a long time to find/secure new sites; 4) slower-than-expected

development of its CPO business; 5) shorter-than-expected testing time leading to lower demand for its products; 6) weak
adoption of high-premium cold plate products; and 7) increasing competition from new entrants.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

## Document 094: 260921_daiwa_winway

Source PDF: all report pdf/260921_daiwa_winway.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-21
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide an update on Winway’s business outlook.

What’s the impact: Marked revenue upside, but a more conservative 
gross margin outlook. We raise our 3Q26 revenue forecast by 9.3% to 
TWD5.0bn (+177.8% YoY) to reflect the stronger-than-expected execution 
of capacity expansion at Renwu Phase 1. Management guided for flat QoQ 
revenue in 4Q26, due to: 1) seasonality; 2) existing capacity being nearly 
fully utilised, with additional capacity scheduled to come online in 1H27; 
and 3) potential revenue recognition delays into 1H27 to accommodate 
customers’ schedules. On profitability, we lower our 3Q26E gross margin 
by 1.8pp, as we expect higher revenue contribution from probe cards and 
incremental depreciation from the new plant to dilute margins. Overall, for 
2026E and 2027E, we raise our revenue by 3.2% and 0.9%, while lowering 
our gross margin by 1.2pp and 1.7pp, respectively.

Probe card share concerns appear overdone. In response to concerns 
over probe card order losses, we believe the expanding TAM will attract 
competitors; the key differentiator is whether suppliers can scale capacity 
to meet customers’ demands. Winway does not expect its probe card share 
at its largest customer to decline and remains confident in maintaining its 
market-leading position. Its Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems (MEMS) 
probe card supply agreement with Technoprobe (TPRO IM, Not Rated) 
remains in place, which should support its technology competitiveness.

Socket capacity expansion remains on track. Winway doubled its socket 
capacity YoY in 2026 and remains on track to reach monthly pogo-pin 
capacity of 14m units by 1H27, per management. We estimate monthly 
capacity to grow further to 16-18m units by end-2027. Management’s long-
term target is to raise its pogo-pin self-sufficiency ratio to 50-60%, which 
should support gross margin expansion through greater vertical integration.

What we recommend: We slightly raise revenue top-line but lower our 
2026-28E EPS by 1-5% to factor in conservative gross margin 
assumptions. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call, with 12M TP of TWD10,000, 
based on a PER of 50x, applied to our 2027E forward EPS. Our 2026-28E 
EPS CAGR of 100% makes our target PER undemanding, given that the 
resulting PEG ratio is 0.5x. Key downside risks: intensifying competition.

How we differ: Our revised 2026-27E EPS are now roughly in line with 
consensus, reflecting a more prudent gross margin. While market noise 
persists, we see no structural threat to Winway's leading position in sockets 
and probe cards.

21 September 2026

Capacity ramp drives revenue upside

➢ Revenue upside with more prudent gross margin assumptions 
➢ Probe card position intact; socket expansion on track 
➢ Reaffirming Buy (1) rating with an unchanged 12M TP of TWD10,000

Company profile 
Founded in Kaohsiung in 2001, WinWay has evolved from an optoelectronic test-fixture supplier into a 
leading logic test-interface provider. Its portfolio includes test sockets, MEMS probe cards and contact 
elements. Growth is driven by increasing AI/HPC testing complexity, GPU and ASIC design wins, 
expanding SLT adoption and next-generation HyperSocket solutions.

### Page 1

ab 23 September 2026
Global Research
Key Call: MediaTek Inc.
Multiple catalysts ahead on TPU; quantifying
upside in new ASIC projects
Stronger TPU upside in 2028-29E
We believe the market has not fully priced in MediaTek's strategic value and opportunity
in Google TPU, and view several catalysts ahead. We keep TPU sales at US$18bn in
2027E, but raise 2028/29E to US$43.5bn/52.5bn from 35.0/40.0bn previously, as we
factor in longer product cycle of v8t in 2028E and stronger growth of v9 in 2029E. See

our TPU financial analysis in Figure 1U B S  f o r e c a s t s  f o r   M e d ia T e k ' s  T P U  b u s in e s s . Catalysts in next 6-9 months include 1) v9 tape out
delivery by Nov 2026E, 2) improving visibility and execution of Intel EMIB-T, 3) Google
further raising v9 volume forecast in H127 assuming constructive feedback from v9
system verification, 4) MediaTek upgrading TPU guidance during their earnings
conference in Oct, Jan/Feb.
MediaTek potentially adding CoWoS on top of EMIB-T to ramp more v9 in 2028-

29
We are increasingly more optimistic about MediaTek's TPU v9 volume potential in 2028-
29. Overall execution of Google and MediaTek appears smoother for v9 compared with
v8t, and the tape out is so far on track for delivery by Nov 2026. On EMIB-T, our deep
dive suggests better than feared execution for substrate manufacturing at Ibiden, with
increasing support from Unimicron, Shinko, and more suppliers. EMIB-T supply should
be sufficient to drive >2m v9 volume in 2028. However, EMIB-T substrate could be tight

if v9 volume grows to 4m or more in 2029. We believe that, likely due to supply
consideration, MediaTek could be engaging with TSMC to add a CoWoS solution for v9.
It's still early stage, but could reflect the meaningful upside potential for v9 into 2029.
Upside risk from new ASIC projects
MediaTek has been proactively exploring new projects to further scale ASICs sales. There
are two projects with higher possibility, although not yet finalized. 1) SpaceX's Dojo 3

based on TSMC N2 and SoW (System on Wafer) packaging. MediaTek could be
collaborating with Nvidia to offer NVLink Fusion to secure this project. 2) Tesla's AI6 for
ADAS and robotics, which could be first produced at Intel fab, followed by Terafab. Tesla
is already deploying AI6 at TSMC and Samsung Foundry, but the goal could be to move
more volume to Terafab if the ramp is successful. Our initial view is both projects
combined could drive up to US$10-15bn ASIC revenue opportunity for MediaTek. We

expect both projects to be finalized soon in coming months given the goal to enter mass
production by 2029.
Valuation: reiterate Key Call Buy; raising price target to NT$7,300
We fine tune 2027E EPS on softer smartphone outlook but raise 2028/29E EPS by
21/28%. Our 2028 EPS estimate is 44% above consensus. Raise price target to NT
$7,300 from NT$6,500 based on unchanged 27x 2027-28E PE. Reiterate Key Call Buy.

Company Description
MediaTek is a leading integrated circuit (IC) design company in Taiwan, focusing on solutions
from the edge to the cloud, applications for its product offerings range from smartphones,
smart homes and AI PCs to high-performance computing, automotive, and AI data centers.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target for MediaTek is based on a target PE methodology.
Fabless integrated circuit (IC) design houses face a variety of uncertainties, including rapidly
changing technology, end-market demand, competition, employee turnover and foundry
wafer costs. They are also vulnerable to legal disputes over intellectual property rights. In
addition, MediaTek is exposed to potential price competition in handset SoC, TV SoC, etc.

Downside risks include slower-than-expected market share gains and a weaker-than-
expected demand recovery.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

## Document 104: 260923_daiwa_Topoint Technology

Source PDF: all report pdf/260923_daiwa_Topoint Technology.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-23
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Background: Topoint was among the world’s top 3 PCB drill-bit suppliers 
by revenue and sales volume in 2025. It supplies drill bits and router bits to 
PCB manufacturers and expanded into PCB drilling services in 2010. In 
2Q26, drill bits accounted for 68% of revenue, while drilling services 
accounted for 32%. It operates drill-bit manufacturing facilities and drilling-
service sites in Taiwan, China and Thailand.

Highlights: AI PCB upgrades are driving drill-bit market growth. AI 
PCBs increasingly feature 30+ layers, M8-grade CCL or higher-grade 
materials, and denser routing, driving demand for PCB drill bits in 3 ways: 
1) more interconnects increase the number of holes to be drilled; 2) thicker, 
harder boards accelerate bit wear and raise consumption; and 3) more 
demanding drilling requirements support greater adoption of high-end bits. 
Frost & Sullivan forecasts the global PCB drill-bit market to grow at a 9.6% 
CAGR in 2026-30, outpacing the global PCB market.

Customer investment underscores tight drill-bit supply. With drill-bit 
capacity running at near-full utilisation, management expects monthly drill-
bit capacity to rise from 32m units at end-2025 to 45-50m at end-2026, 70-
75m at end-2027 and 90-100m at end-2028. Notably, key customers 
Unimicron (3037 TT, TWD1,160, Buy [1], covered by Sheng Cheng), GCE 
(2368 TT, TWD1,140, Buy [1], covered by Sheng Cheng) and Zhen Ding 
(4958 TT, not rated) have participated in Topoint’s private convertible-bond 
offering, providing funding for its expansion.

Coated drill bits and high-aspect-ratio drilling driving spec upgrades. 
Tungsten is a key raw material for PCB drill bits, with Topoint sourcing it 
based on plant location. Topoint adjusts prices to reflect changes in costs 
and raised prices by 20% in 1H26. Alongside price hikes, a richer product 
mix lifted 2Q26 gross margin to a record high, up 5.2pp QoQ and 12.3pp 
YoY. As PCBs become harder, demand is shifting towards coated drill bits. 
Coatings such as diamond-like carbon improve wear resistance and bit life, 
while helping to maintain hole-wall quality and drilling yields. High-end 
coated products accounted for 56% of the drill-bit shipment mix in 1H26, up 
from 48% in 2025. Thicker boards and smaller hole diameters are also 
driving a shift towards micro drill bits with high aspect ratios, supporting 
ASPs and margins. Looking ahead, next-gen CCL materials present further 
drilling challenges: PTFE can adhere to the bit during machining, while 
quartz fibre accelerates wear. Per Topoint, these spec upgrades will sustain 
product-mix improvement and support higher margins over the long term.

Valuation: Topoint is currently trading at 17.7/13.8x 2027/28E PER based 
on Bloomberg consensus (vs. its average 1-year forward PER of 27x).

23 September 2026

A key supplier riding the drill-bit upcycle

➢ Top-3 global drill-bit supplier expanding capacity with customer backing 
➢ Ongoing high-end drill-bit upgrades to sustain margin expansion 
➢ Currently trading at 18x/14x 2027/28E PER based on consensus

Share price performance

Source: FactSet, Daiwa forecasts 
Topoint Technology (8021 TT)
Target price: n.a.

公司簡介 
陽明海運是全球第九大航商，2026/9 運力
77.3 萬 TEU 約 99 艘，市佔率 2.2%，加入
THE Alliance 聯盟 。 安裝脫硫塔比重超過6
成 ， 自有櫃佔54%。1H26 年美洲線佔營收
比重 36%，歐洲線佔 39%，近洋佔 13%，
其他航線(中東、南美等)佔 12%，運送量
223.6 萬 TEU(+4%YoY)，平均運價 1012
美元/TEU(-5%YoY)。

主要客戶： 
主要競爭對手：長榮、萬海

陳宜里 yili.chen@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

陽明 (2609 TT) 
旺季獲利上修，淡季運價有撐

永豐觀點 
亞洲港口擁擠、船舶繞航、巴拿馬運河水位低下降低供給周轉支撐淡季運價有撐。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：(1) 3Q 美洲線運價優於預期旺季獲利佳，巴拿馬運河水位低下支撐 4Q
運價相對高檔，(2) 亞洲線進入旺季相對暢旺，(3) 歷史 PBR 落在 0.5-4.3 倍，目
前 PBR 為 0.54 倍 ， 評價處於相對低檔 ， 維持買進 ， 目標價由71 元(0.7X2026BVPS)
調整到 73 元(0.65 X2027 BVPS)。

ESG 評析 
陽明企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A+等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

陽明為全球第九大海運業者：陽明海運是全球第九大航商，2026/9 運力 77.3 萬
TEU 約 99 艘(較 2025 年底的 71.4 萬 TEU 成長 8.3%)，市佔率 2.2%，加入 THE 
Alliance 聯盟 (聯盟成 員之 一赫伯羅特2025/1 退出 ，聯盟更名為 Premier 
Alliance(2025/2-2030/1)。安裝脫硫塔比重超過 6 成，自有櫃佔 54%。1H26 年
美洲線佔營收比重 36%，歐洲線佔 39%，近洋佔 13%，其他航線(中東、南美等)
佔 12%，運送量 223.6 萬 TEU(+4%YoY)，平均運價 1012 美元/TEU(-5%YoY)。
股權方面，官股持有 31%。 
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 為 5.37 元：受惠美洲線與亞洲線運價上漲，加上 LNG 船投
入地中海航線 ， 預估營收656 億元(+56%YoY)， 稅後獲利187.5 億元(+210%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 為 5.37 元。4Q 歐洲線進入淡季、美洲線受巴拿馬運河水位降低相對有
撐、亞洲進入旺季加上塞港相對強勢，公司認為4Q 呈現緩步修正，研究部預估營
收 573 億元(+53%YoY)，稅後獲利 136.4 億元(+496%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 3.91
元。 
燃油成本的影響：陽明 1H26 年燃油成本佔 16%，6 月油價攀升至 692 美元/噸，
整體燃油成本較去年同期+1%。2Q 高硫油平均 617 美元/噸(+23.5%QoQ)，低硫
油平均 775 美元/噸(+24.5%QoQ)，因地緣政治影響，3Q 高硫油平均 562 美元/
噸-9%QoQ)，低硫油平均 792 美元/噸(+2.3%QoQ)，研究部假設陽明年耗油量
142 萬噸，船用燃油平均一年漲 100 美元/噸，燃油成本增加 44 億元，EPS 影響
1.26 元，不過航商透過燃油附加費的徵收以分散燃油成本上漲的壓力。

### Page 3

3

2026 年供給成長是近幾年最低一年：巴拿馬運河當局 9/4 將每天通行艘樹從 36
艘降至 34 艘，9/15 再降到 32 艘，目前運河水位較去年低 2.5%，預期 10 月水位
將低於去年同期 3.8%，艙位供給將再度限縮。無論是 Drewry，Clarkson 或是
Alphaliner 預估 2026 年船舶供給成長率是近幾年最低的 ， 加上紅海改道的船舶回
歸延遲，有效運力釋放再度放緩，2026 年是供給相對較小的一年。 
3Q26 第 2 艘雙燃料船交船：新船在手訂單 23 艘約 30 萬 TEU，1Q26-4Q26 年
將交 5 艘 1.56 萬 TEU LNG 船(已交 4 艘)，3Q28-2Q29 交 7 艘 1.6 萬 TEU LNG
雙燃料船，4Q28-1Q30 交 6 艘 8 千 TEU 甲醇雙燃料船，4Q28-3Q29 年交 6 艘
1.3 萬 TEU LNG 雙燃料船。轉投資光明海運則預計 3Q28-2Q29 交 4 艘 6.4 萬
DWT 散裝船。 
現金殖利率 ：假設明年現金股利配發 4 成 ， 現金股利4.53 元 ， 現金殖利率為7.4%。

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操
作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預
期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

個股報告 
嘉晶(3016) 
8 吋 Si Epi 滿載支撐獲利，Ge/Si 擴產加速 
投資評等 目標價 
區間操作 $152 
前次投資建議：2026.04.30 
「區間操作」 ，目標價: $92

報告日期：2026.09.23 研 究 員：李彝安

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 嘉晶 26Q2 毛利率 23.5%優於預期，8吋 Si Epi 維持滿載，Ge/Si需求能見度延伸至 2028 年，
上修 2026/27F EPS 至 2.56/3.69，目標價 152 元(6.5x2027(F)PBR)，潛在上漲空間 14.7%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/09/22 收盤價： 132.50 ➢ 26Q2 營收 13.09 億元，季增 23.6%、年增 37.1%，毛
利率 23.5%、營益率16.9%，稅後淨利1.88 億元、EPS 
0.65 元。營收僅略高於研究部原估12.92 億元，毛利率
較原估 18.4%高出 5.1ppt，主要反映8吋產品占比提高
及稼動率改善，因此上修獲利。 
➢ 26H1 8 吋 Si Epi 產能滿載，8吋占 Si Epi 營收比重由
去年同期 59%提升至 71%，MOSFET 與 PMIC 晶圓產
出年增 50%。既有產能仍無法滿足客戶需求，2026 年
新增設備預計自 2027 年陸續開出。 
➢ Compound Epi 營運改善，26H1 GaN/SiC營收分別年
增 20%/28%；6 吋 GaN 已轉虧為盈且產能滿載，SiC
則受惠日本 IDM 客戶自 26Q2 開始放量，稼動率提高
後虧損明顯收斂。 Ge/Si PD Epi 26Q2 營收季增
113%，26H2 營收預估較 26H1 成長 2.5 倍，2027 年
產能預計增至目前 3.5～4倍，訂單能見度延伸至2028
年；惟 26H1 營收占比僅約 1%，研究部推估 2027 年
占比仍約 4%～5%，短期 EPS貢獻有限。 
➢ 研究部上修 2026 年營收至 53.3 億元、毛利率 23.1%、
EPS 2.56元；2027 年營收 68.7 億元、毛利率25.1%、
EPS 3.69元 。 考量8吋 Si Epi維持滿載 、Compound Epi
獲利改善及 Ge/Si後續成長，給予 6.5x 27F PBR，目
標價 152 元。6.5x 已位於公司過去約 2–8x PBR 區間
偏高位置，反映目前獲利改善及成長能見度提升，評價
進一步上修空間有限；潛在上漲空間 14.7%，維持「區
間操作」評等。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析 
嘉晶為國內少數同時跨足矽磊晶、化合物半導體與矽光子之專業磊晶代工廠 
嘉晶（3016）成立於 1996 年，總部位於新竹科學園區，為國內專業半導體
磊晶 （Epitaxy） 代工廠，主要股東為漢民科技集團。公司早期以矽磊晶 （Silicon 
Epi）代工起家，產品應用於電源管理 IC（PMIC）、MOSFET 等功率半導
體領域；中期切入化合物半導體磊晶，建置氮化鎵（GaN）與碳化矽（SiC）
產線，跨入射頻與功率元件市場，下游應用涵蓋資料中心電源、電動車、再
生能源、人形機器人與航太科技；近期則進一步切入矽光子（ Silicon 
Photonics）用 Ge on Si（鍺在矽上）光偵測器（PD）磊晶代工，為國內少
數同時具備三大磊晶平台量產能力之專業業者。 
公司於台灣設有 4 吋、5 吋、6 吋、8 吋多尺寸磊晶產線，磊晶製程涵蓋  
Silicon、GaN、SiC、Ge on Si 等多種材料系統，具備磊晶配方設計、設備
運轉、品質與良率管控之完整 Know-How。客戶結構上，下游涵蓋 Fabless 
設計業者與 IDM 廠，訂單來源以台灣為主，日本與其他地區次之。

圖一、嘉晶(3016) 2026H1產品別營收比重

資料來源：嘉晶，兆豐投顧彙整

- 3 - 
個股報告 
26Q2 毛利率優於預期，上修 2026年獲利 
嘉晶 26Q2 營收 13.09 億元，季增 23.6%、年增 37.1%，毛利率 23.5%，季
增 5.7ppt，營益率 16.9%，稅後淨利 1.88 億元、EPS 0.65 元；26H1 營收
23.68億元 、 年增27.9%， 毛利率20.9%，EPS 1.00元 ， 較去年同期EPS -0.14
元明顯改善。 
26Q2營收與研究部原估 12.92億元差異有限 ， 惟毛利率23.5%較原估 18.4%
高出 5.1ppt，獲利優於預期主要來自產品組合及稼動率改善。26H1 持續將
小尺寸產能轉往 8 吋，8 吋產品占比提高，加上 8 吋產線維持滿載，帶動固
定成本吸收及整體獲利改善。26Q2 毛利率尚未反映漲價效益，價格調整自
26Q3 才開始，26H2 將陸續反映效益。  
研究部將 2026 年營收由前次 51.0 億元上修至 53.3 億元，年增 37.0%；毛
利率由 18.1%上修至 23.1%，稅後淨利 7.6億元、EPS由 1.70 元上修至 2.56
元。預估 26Q3/26Q4 營收 14.59/15.06 億元，季增 11.5%/3.2%，毛利率
24.6%/24.9%，EPS均為 0.8元。

8吋 Si Epi維持滿載，2027年新增產能陸續開出 
Si Epi 仍為嘉晶主要營收與獲利來源，應用涵蓋 PMIC、Power MOSFET、
IGBT、Driver 及 Protection IC 等功率元件。AI 伺服器功率密度提升，帶動
PMIC與 Power MOSFET 需求增加，26H1 8吋 Si Epi產能滿載，營收占 Si 
Epi比重由去年同期 59%提升至 71%，MOSFET與 PMIC晶圓產出年增50%。 
國內部分晶圓廠原先具備自有 Epi產能，但AI 相關 PMIC需求增加後，既有
產能不足，帶動委外需求增加。26H2 Si Epi 營收預估年增約 42%，8 吋既
有產能滿載後仍無法滿足客戶需求，目前客戶持續要求擴產，2027 年新增產
能開出後仍為主要營收成長來源。 
AI伺服器功率需求亦帶動不同材料平台成長，其中Si Epi主要對應 PMIC 與
MOSFET；800V高壓直流架構則增加 GaN及 SiC等化合物半導體的應用機
會 。 三項產品雖均受惠AI電源需求 ， 但目前對嘉晶獲利的貢獻仍以8吋 Si Epi
為主。

26Q3 開始調價，26H2營收與獲利優於 26H1 
原物料及相關成本上升，自 26Q3 開始針對不同產品與客戶調整價格。由於
價格調整自 26Q3 才開始 ，26Q2 毛利率由 17.8%提升至 23.5%主要仍反映 8
吋占比及產能利用率改善，而非 ASP上升。26H2 Si Epi 需求維持高檔，加
上價格調整開始反映，研究部預估26Q3/26Q4 毛利率分別為 24.6%/24.9%，
高於 26Q2 的 23.5%。26H2 營收 29.65 億元，較 26H1 成長 25.2%，2026
全年營收 53.3 億元、年增 37.0%，符合公司全年營收年增>35%的展望。 
2027 年毛利率預估進一步提升至 25.1%，主要反映產品組合持續改善、
Compound Epi 獲利回升，以及 Ge/Si較高毛利產品占比增加；另一方面，
2026 年新增設備的折舊將自 2027～28年陸續認列，部分抵銷產品組合改善
效益，因此並未假設 Si Epi毛利率持續大幅上升。

圖二、嘉晶(3016)各季營收及獲利走勢預估

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐投顧預估

Compound Epi營運改善，GaN轉盈、SiC虧損收斂 
26H1 Compound Epi 營運明顯改善，GaN營收年增 20%、SiC年增 28%。
GaN 自 25Q3 至 26Q2 連續四季成長，6 吋產能目前已滿載並由虧轉盈，8
吋則開始小量生產；現有 6 吋需求高於可供產能，客戶有對公司提出擴產需
求。GaN 後續擴產狀況須配合客戶產品平台及下游 Foundry 進度。目前 6
吋訂單充足，但長期產業逐步往 8吋發展，因此新增產能將依客戶與Foundry
合作成熟度調整。SiC 方面，日本 IDM客戶自 26Q2 開始放量，帶動產能利
用率回升，原先投資虧損明顯縮小。中國SiC市場仍面臨較激烈的價格競爭，
目前嘉晶業務以日本及其他非中國市場為主。

26H2 Compound Epi 營收預估年增 86%，改善幅度高於 Si Epi。惟現階段
Compound Epi營收規模仍低於 Si Epi，2026～27年對獲利的主要貢獻在於
GaN由虧轉盈及 SiC虧損持續收斂，而非取代 Si Epi成為主要獲利來源。

Compound Epi 營運改善，GaN轉盈、SiC虧損收斂 
Ge/Si PD Epi主要應用於高速光通訊接收端 Photodiode，目前對應800G 及
1.6T 可插拔式光模組。26Q2 營收季增 113%，26H2 營收預估較 26H1成長
約 2.5 倍。。需求端能見度已延伸至 2028 年，目前產能只能滿足既有客戶
不到 50%的需求。2027 年產能預計增至目前 3.5～4倍，但依現有訂單需求
仍不足，後續仍有追加設備空間；主要擴產限制來自設備交期。

Ge/Si成長率雖高，但短期對整體EPS貢獻有限。26H1營收占比僅約 1%，
依 26H2 成長幅度及 2027 年 3.5～4 倍成長假設推估，2027 年營收約 3 億

元，占研究部 2027F 營收 68.7 億元約 4%～5%。即使 Ge/Si 毛利率明顯高
於一般 Epi， 基期仍小，2026～27年公司獲利仍主要取決於 8吋 Si Epi營收、
稼動率及毛利率。

擴產效益自 2027年陸續反映，獲利延續成長 
研究部預估 2026 年營收 53.3 億元、年增 37.0%，毛利率 23.1%、營益率
16.7%，稅後淨利 7.60 億元、EPS 2.56 元；2027 年營收 68.7 億元、年增
28.8%，毛利率 25.1%、營益率 19.1%，稅後淨利 10.95 億元、EPS 3.69
元。 
2026 年獲利上修主要反映 26Q2 毛利率優於預期、26H2 8 吋 Si Epi 維持高
稼動率及 26Q3 開始調價；2027 年則受惠 8 吋新增產能陸續開出、GaN 獲
利增加與 SiC 虧損收斂，以及 Ge/Si營收占比提升。 
估值延續採用 PBR評價。嘉晶過去 PBR約落在 2～8x，考量 8吋 Si Epi 供
需改善、Compound Epi 營運轉佳及 Ge/Si中長期成長動能，給予 6.5x 27F 
PBR。6.5x 位於歷史區間偏高位置，已反映部分基本面改善與 2027 年成長
預期，後續評價倍數上修空間相對有限。上調目標價至 152 元(6.5x 27 (F) 
PBR)，潛在上漲空間 14.7%，維持「區間操作」評等。
